### Loan Payment Calculator
01 . | =+'Model Inputs'!B1 | . | . | . | . | .
02 . | =+'Model Inputs'!B2 | . | . | . | . | .
03 . | Loan payment calculator | . | . | . | . | .
04 . | . | . | . | . | . | .
05 . | Annual interest rate | 0.05 | . | Note: This calculator can generate principal and interest payments for a period of up to 30 years (360 months). | . | .
06 . | Monthly rate | =(1+C5)^(1/12)-1 | . | . | . | .
07 . | Loan amount | =+'Model Inputs'!C44 | . | . | . | .

10 . | . | . | . | . | . | .
11 . | . | Month | Principal balance | Principal payment | Interest | Payment
12 . | . | 1 | =C7 | =G12-F12 | =-$C$6*D12 | =IF(C12>$C$8,0,$C$9)
13 . | . | 2 | =D12+E12 | =G13-F13 | =-$C$6*D13 | .
